We then extract the actor, compress it one stage at a time, and evaluate it on five driving curricula. We show that structured pruning is the stage at which the driving capability is first lost. Meanwhile, distillation improves the pruned actor, but the improvement is limited by its rehearsal data. Integer quantization of the improved actor loses some of the curricula that require the vehicle to stop and then resume. Interestingly, the same procedure on the unpruned actor preserves all five curricula. Our study thus provides an empirical analysis aiming to answer the currently active discussions on how to accept a compressed driving policy, so as to achieve a safe and statistically reliable deployment of automated driving functions.

</details>


### [97] [SOVER: Formal Certification of Optimization Reformulations via LLM-Assisted SMT Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00728)
*Swapnil Bhattacharyya,Mayank Baranwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种将LLM映射与SMT证据分离的验证流程（SOVER），结合Z3与dReal，能在新的NLEquiv-150基准上几乎完美鉴别等价/非等价重写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单靠求解器运行不能可靠地验证两个建模语言表述的等价性，因求解器结果易受局部极值、时间超限、数值伪影与语义微差影响，需形式化、容差感知的验证方法。

Method: 用LLM抽取变量与约束之间的映射（语义映射），对线性与整数子类用Z3验证域可行性与目标序关系，对连续非线性用dReal执行容忍度感知的可行性/范围与ε-argmin检查，从而实现形式化判定。

Result: 构建SOVER框架并发布NLEquiv-150基准（100对等价，50对难的非等价），在LLM给出映射的条件下对150对样例正确分类149对（含全部50个难例），唯一错误归因于映射提取不完整。

Conclusion: SOVER有效地将LLM提取的语义映射与形式化SMT证实分离，结合Z3与dReal实现对混合整数线性与连续非线性重写的严格等价/非等价判定，实验证明其在NLEquiv-150基准上几乎全对（149/150）。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable promise in translating and reformulating complex mathematical optimization problems across modeling languages. However, validating such transformations through empirical solver executions alone is unreliable, as solver outcomes may be affected by local minima, structural timeouts, numerical artifacts, and subtle semantic divergence between formulations. We introduce SOVER, an LLM-assisted SMT framework that separates semantic mapping from formal certification: Z3 checks domain cross-feasibility and global objective-order preservation for mixed-integer linear formulations, while dReal provides tolerance-aware feasibility/range and $ε$-argmin checks for continuous nonlinear formulations. We also introduce NLEquiv-150, a public benchmark of 100 equivalent and 50 deliberately hard non-equivalent nonlinear reformulation pairs. With LLM-extracted mappings, SOVER classifies 149/150 pairs (99.33%) correctly, including all 50 hard negatives; the sole error is an incomplete mapping extraction.

</details>


### [98] [Agentic Empirical Asset Pricing: Methodological Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00731)
*Yingjian Pan,Xiaowei Ding,Kay Giesecke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Empirical Asset Pricing（AEAP）范式，构建参考架构与严格的样本外因子发现评估方法；实证比较SEADS与五个基线表明需多维度评估与检验发现过程的可靠性，并揭示若干限制与负面发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理人带来自动化科学发现的能力，应将自动发现系统本身作为研究对象，而非仅回测其生成的因子或交易；为此需要完整的架构与严格的样本外评估标准来测量发现过程的可重复性、鲁棒性和实用性。

Method: 构建AEAP框架与参考架构，设计严格的因子评估标准与样本外回测方法，复现并实现五个基线以及SEADS，在两个US股票面板上进行静态输出评估及滚动重执行（rolling re-execution）来检验发现系统的稳定性，比较多项评估指标并分析失败与局限。

Result: 提出AEAP定义与核心组件，给出因子发现参考架构与评估标准，并提出样本外回测发现系统的方法。实证部分：在两个美股面板上将SEADS与五个基线比较，发现无单一评价指标稳定优胜，滚动重执行揭示发现过程的可靠性问题，并报告若干负面结果与局限，提示未来AEAP评估的陷阱。

Conclusion: AEAP提出了利用LLM代理人自动化资产定价研究的新范式，并强调需同时评估自动发现系统本身与其输出。本文提出了AEAP定义、核心构件、因子发现参考架构和严格的评估标准，并提出了用于对发现系统进行样本外回测的方法。通过将SEADS与五个基线在两个美股面板上比较，发现没有单一指标能一致排名，需多维度评估；滚动重执行检验了发现过程的可靠性；还报告了若干负面发现和局限。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents enable a new paradigm for asset pricing, which we call Agentic Empirical Asset Pricing (AEAP): systems that autonomously conduct the scientific discovery process itself. We define AEAP and identify its core building blocks. Existing evaluation practices backtest only the outputs (factors or trades), not the autonomous discovery system that produced them. We focus on factor discovery, contributing a reference architecture, a rigorous evaluation standard for discovered factors, and a method for out-of-sample backtesting the discovery system. As a concrete instance of that architecture, we evaluate SEADS against five re-implemented baselines on two US equity panels using this standard: no single metric ranks the systems consistently, motivating evaluation on multiple axes at once. A separate rolling re-execution then asks the complementary question of whether the discovery process itself, not one static output, is reliable. We also report negative findings and limitations that surface further evaluation pitfalls for future AEAP systems.

</details>


### [99] [Escaping Redundant Reasoning: Structure-Aware Search for Inference-Time LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00738)
*Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BASIN is a lightweight, training-free method that clusters reasoning states into basins and penalizes repeated visits to promote genuinely diverse inference-time search; it improves over Tree of Thoughts and is explained via a new diagnostic metric Δ.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Inference-time search with LLMs often over-concentrates on structurally or semantically similar reasoning trajectories, missing alternative correct strategies—this failure mode is called reasoning basin collapse. A selection mechanism that is aware of state structure can diversify search more effectively under fixed compute.

Method: Training-free, structure-aware selection: cluster reasoning states into basins (based on structure/semantics), apply a penalty for revisiting previously explored basins during inference-time search, and allocate search budget to under-explored basins. QA-BASIN augments this with a quality-aware mechanism that preserves basins with high-scoring candidates to avoid over-diversification. Evaluation compares BASIN and QA-BASIN vs Tree of Thoughts under matched inference budgets, and introduces redundancy gap Δ as a diagnostic metric.

Result: Under equal compute budgets, BASIN yields substantial empirical gains (up to +22 percentage points on Game of 24, +6.7 pp on MuSR) over ToT; QA-BASIN further stabilizes performance by retaining high-quality basins when unconditional diversification hurts. BASIN systematically increases the redundancy gap Δ (i.e., search concentrates differently for correct vs incorrect answers), indicating better allocation of search effort. Code is released.

Conclusion: BASIN mitigates inference-time "reasoning basin collapse" by grouping reasoning states into basins and penalizing repeated exploration of the same basin, thereby reallocating limited compute to more diverse and genuinely distinct reasoning trajectories. This structure-aware, training-free selection consistently improves reasoning performance over baselines (notably Tree of Thoughts) and can be made robust via a quality-aware variant (QA-BASIN). The introduced redundancy gap Δ explains when basin-aware selection helps: positive shifts in Δ correlate with better correctness.

Abstract: Inference-time search with large language models (LLMs) often concentrates on a small set of structurally or semantically similar trajectories, leaving alternatives underexplored---a failure mode we call \textit{reasoning basin collapse}. We introduce BASIN, a training-free, structure-aware selection method that groups reasoning states into basins and penalizes repeated visits to the same strategy, thereby reallocating search across genuinely distinct reasoning paths under a fixed compute budget. Under matched inference budgets, BASIN improves over Tree of Thoughts (ToT) by up to $+22$pp on Game of 24 and $+6.7$pp on MuSR. A quality-aware variant, QA-BASIN, further improves robustness by preserving high-quality basins when unconditional diversification over-explores. To explain when basin-aware selection helps, we introduce the redundancy gap $Δ$, which measures how differently search concentrates for correct versus incorrect predictions: standard ToT often operates near $Δ\approx 0$, while BASIN consistently shifts $Δ$ positive. More broadly, BASIN suggests structure-aware selection as a simple and general approach to improving inference-time reasoning. Code can be found at https://github.com/GitHubLuCheng/basin.

</details>


### [100] [ContextPipe: Database-Inspired Context Assembly for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00749)
*Peng Xu,Zuyu Zhang,Yuze Sun,Feng Tian,Long Wang,Chen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Treat context assembly like database query execution; ContextPipe’s pipeline reduces token usage, LLM calls, and latency while adding auditability and isolation, at a cache-hit trade-off.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Runtime context assembly for LLM agents must select, order, and compact information under hard context-window budgets and tiered byte-sensitive caches, but current implementations are fragmented and ad hoc in production systems.

Method: Introduce ContextPipe: a five-phase pipeline (Plan, Bind, Optimize, Execute, Feedback), backed by a structured data-source catalog, a deterministic cache-aware optimizer, and EXPLAIN ANALYZE tracing; compare against append-only context construction.

Result: On SWE-bench Pro Qutebrowser subset, ContextPipe reduced total token volume by 31%, LLM calls by 23%, and response time by 9%, while lowering KV cache-hit ratio compared to append-only policy.

Conclusion: ContextPipe makes context assembly for long-horizon LLM agents systematic and performant by treating it like relational query execution, yielding auditable, replayable, failure-isolated pipelines.

Abstract: Long-horizon large language model (LLM) agents require context assembly: the runtime must decide what to include in each prompt, in what order, and when to compact history under a hard context-window budget and a byte-sensitive prompt cache. In production agentic systems, this logic is scattered across prompt builders, ad hoc compaction routines, cache-break workarounds, and per-provider shims. We argue that context assembly is structurally isomorphic to query execution in a relational database: both execute under a hard budget, exploit a tiered cache, and leverage statistics. We adopt this discipline in ContextPipe: a five-phase pipeline (Plan Bind Optimize Execute Feedback) backed by a structured data-source catalog, a deterministic cache-aware optimizer, and an EXPLAIN ANALYZE trace. We show that context in ContextPipe is auditable, replayable, and failure-isolated. A preliminary evaluation using the SWE-bench Pro Qutebrowser subset shows that, compared with the append-only context construction policy, ContextPipe reduces total token volume by 31%, LLM calls by 23%, and response time by 9%, at the cost of a lower KV cache-hit ratio.

</details>


### [101] [S^3martCirc: Self-supervised Smart Circuit Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00755)
*Wendy Zheng,Yinhan He,Liang Wu,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Proposes S^3martCirc, a self-supervised framework that jointly discovers circuits and interprets node functionality with two general roles and a quantitative metric, improving circuit discovery


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current two-stage MI methods treat circuit discovery and functional interpretation separately, but importance and function are codependent; need unified approach with generalizable roles and quantitative assignment

Method: Mechanistic interpretability for LLMs

Result: S^3martCirc outperforms existing methods in circuit discovery through joint discovery and interpretation using two general computational roles and a quantitative metric

Conclusion: Unified discovery and interpretation with generalizable roles and quantitative metrics enables better circuit discovery performance than previous sequential methods.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across diverse tasks, from text summarization to question answering. Despite these capabilities, their black-box nature obscures internal decision-making processes. Mechanistic interpretability (MI) aims to address this by reverse-engineering neural networks into human-understandable algorithms. Current MI approaches for LLMs typically follow a two-stage paradigm: first identifying important components (circuit discovery), where components are typically individual nodes such as an attention head or feedforward neuron, and second determining the role they play in a certain task (functional interpretation). However, this sequential approach overlooks a fundamental insight: a component's importance and its functional role are inherently codependent. Unifying these stages presents two key challenges: (1) functional roles are often tied to specific nodes or components, limiting generalization, and (2) their identification relies on subjective interpretation rather than quantifiable metrics. To address these challenges, we propose S^3martCirc (Self-supervised Smart Circuit Discovery), a unified framework that simultaneously discovers circuits and interprets functionality. S^3martCirc abstracts node behavior into two general computational roles that generalize across tasks and defines a quantitative metric for assigning them, enabling importance and functional role to be discovered jointly rather than in sequence. Extensive experiments show that our framework outperforms existing methods in circuit discovery.

</details>


### [102] [Automated Tree Knowledge Graph Construction using Ontology Expansion and Retrieval from Vietnamese History Textbooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00763)
*Ket Doan Nguyen,Minh N. H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为越南语教材提出端到端KG构建与层级检索评估流水线，用混合抽取与双模验证控制本体增长，并证实树结构在特定检索场景中优于纯向量检索，但并非对所有查询都有利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥补低资源语言（如越南语）中基于本体扩展的自动KG构建方法缺乏，以及缺乏利用层级结构进行知识检索策略的系统评估。

Method: 三阶段混合关系抽取（Union-Find批内去重、近似跨批搜索、基于大模型的抽取并用质心过滤减少提示），加五步双模型验证防止本体膨胀；设计两层节点架构（不可合并的结构节点与可合并的内容节点）；比较三种图遍历检索策略（Top-Down、Horizontal、Bottom-Up），在合成基准上用NDCG@10等指标评估。

Result: 从约400页越南语高中历史教材构建出750个节点、4341条语义边，本体类型从40增长到41；在109个子图、1210条合成查询上评估，Top-Down（含结构）较向量基线在NDCG@10上提高4.7个百分点，但在无需结构的查询上性能下降。

Conclusion: 提出了一个端到端的面向越南语教材的层级知识图谱构建与检索策略评估流程，证明了树形结构信息在部分检索任务上优于纯向量检索，但在不需要结构上下文的查询上性能会下降。

Abstract: Hierarchical Knowledge graph (KG)-based retrieval augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a powerful approach for supporting large language models with structured knowledge. However, there are primary challenges: (i) the lack of methods for automatic KG construction using ontology expansion for low-resource languages such as Vietnamese, (ii) the absence of systematic evaluation for knowledge retrieval strategies leveraging the hierarchical structures. In this paper, we propose an end-to-end pipeline for KG construction and retrieval strategies evaluation. In the KG construction, we employ a three-phase hybrid relation extraction pipeline: intra-batch deduplication via Union-Find, approximate cross-batch search, and LLM extraction with a centroid filter that reduces prompts combined with a five-step dual-LLM validator to prevent bloated ontology. A two-tier architecture consists of unmergeable structural nodes to preserve the document structure and mergeable content nodes. The retrieval evaluation consists of three graph traversal strategies: Top-Down, Horizontal, and Bottom-Up, which are evaluated on a synthetically generated benchmark of 1,210 Vietnamese queries from 109 subgraphs, categorized by five query directions. In this paper, we construct the tree knowledge graph from Vietnamese high school History textbooks (nearly 400 pages) to produce 750 nodes and 4,341 semantic edges with controlled ontology growth from 40 to 41 types. Among experimental graph traversal strategies, the Top-Down strategy with structure surpasses the vector baseline by 4.7 percentage points in NDCG@10. As a result, tree-structural information provides valuable information beyond flat cosine similarity but degrades performance when the query does not require structural context.

</details>


### [103] [DiagEvo: Diagnosis-Guided Self-Evolution via Hierarchical Error Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00768)
*Xincheng Wei,Yifan Ding,Yoshua Li,Dongsheng Ma,Rongxiang Weng,Xunliang Cai,Wenjian Ding,Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiagEvo无需外部资源，通过从失败历史提取层次化错误原因并据此生成针对性题目与双重置信度过滤，稳定提高多种模型在多个基准上的推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无引导的自我对弈只能用泛化信号（难度、可学性、多样性）生成题目，不能明确指出后续轮次应优先修正哪些未解决的推理弱点；已有有引导方法依赖外部任务资源。作者主张所需方向可以来自求解器自身的失败历史。

Method: 引入Diagnostician模块从失败记录中识别重复错误原因，按技能节点构成层次化错误原因记忆（每个原因标记为Active或Mastered，基于针对性问题上的自洽性），Challenger利用原因的活跃态和出现频次在定向生成与自由探索之间平衡；同时采用双重置信度过滤保留中等难度题目（当最常见答案有明显票数领先时）。

Result: 在三种求解器（Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B、OctoThinker-8B）和九个基准上，使用默认4B diagnostician的DiagEvo在平均准确率上均优于对比基线。在Qwen3-8B上，对五个数学推理基准的平均准确率达72.3%，较R-Zero高4.5个百分点；在全部九个基准上的平均准确率为57.4%，较DARC高1.1个百分点。消融实验显示分层错误记忆和双重置信度过滤都对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: DiagEvo通过从求解器自身失败历史中自动提取并记忆重复错误原因，构建分层的错误原因记忆并据此引导题目生成，从而在无需外部任务资源的情况下稳定改进自我对弈训练效果。

Abstract: Self-play is an effective paradigm for language-model self-evolution, but without guidance, solver performance can plateau or decline across rounds. Unguided methods steer question generation with signals such as difficulty, learnability, or diversity. These signals keep questions challenging and varied but do not specify which unresolved reasoning weaknesses later rounds should target. Guided methods obtain direction from external task resources, including human examples, document corpora, or specified difficulty targets, and therefore rely on task information supplied outside the self-play loop. We show that the needed direction can instead be derived from the solver's own failure history. We introduce DiagEvo, whose diagnostician extracts recurring error causes from this history and stores them in a hierarchical error-cause memory. The memory groups related causes under skill nodes and tracks each as Active or Mastered according to self-consistency on targeted questions. The challenger uses these states and recurrence counts to balance cause-targeted generation with free exploration. Double-confidence filtering retains intermediate-difficulty questions only when the most common solver answer has a clear vote lead. DiagEvo derives its curriculum from information produced during self-play, without external task resources. With the default 4B diagnostician, DiagEvo outperforms every baseline in mean accuracy across all nine benchmarks for each of the three solvers: Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and OctoThinker-8B. On Qwen3-8B, it reaches 72.3% mean accuracy across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, 4.5 percentage points above R-Zero. Its mean accuracy across all nine benchmarks is 57.4%, 1.1 percentage points above DARC. Ablations show that the hierarchical error-cause memory and double-confidence filtering both contribute to these gains.

</details>


### [104] [When Features Become Instances: Inverted Contrastive Learning for Unsupervised Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00782)
*Utsab Ghosh,Roshni Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ICLFS通过把对比学习‘倒置’到特征维并用投影嵌入范数作为显著性评分，结合Laplacian校正，提供了一个在多数基准上优于现有方法的无监督特征选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督特征选择缺乏标签难以定义“有用性”，现有方法多依赖相似性、重构或聚类几何等间接准则；本文提出直接从‘特征表示一致性’出发，将UFS重新表述为特征级的表征学习问题。

Method: 将数据矩阵转置以把每个特征视为样本画像；构造多个掩码正样本视图与一个打乱的负样本视图；在投影器空间用InfoNCE损失学习在这些结构化扰动下的一致表征；使用投影空间嵌入范数对特征打分并以Laplacian门控排序校正去除局部冗余。

Result: 在12个基准数据集上，ICLFS在10个数据集上实现了最佳聚类准确率，并在其余2个数据集上仍保持有竞争力的表现，表明基于特征的对比表示一致性是一个强有力的UFS方案。

Conclusion: 通过在特征维度上应用对比学习并以投影空间嵌入范数作为显著性信号，ICLFS为无监督特征选择提供了一种有效的替代路径，在标准聚类评价协议下在多数基准数据集上达到了最优或接近最优性能。

Abstract: Unsupervised feature selection seeks a compact subset of informative features without access to class labels, making feature utility difficult to define. Existing UFS methods therefore rely on indirect structural criteria, such as similarity preservation, locality, sparsity, cluster geometry, or reconstruction quality. In this paper, we instead study UFS through representation consistency and propose Inverted Contrastive Learning for Unsupervised Feature Selection (ICLFS), a feature-wise contrastive framework that reformulates UFS as a representation learning problem over features rather than samples. ICLFS first inverts the data matrix so that each feature is represented by its sample-profile vector, then constructs multiple masked positive views together with a shuffled negative view, and learns projector-space representations that remain consistent across these structured perturbations under an InfoNCE-based objective. Motivated by recent findings that cosine-based and InfoNCE-based training affect embedding norms, we use projector-space embedding magnitude as the saliency signal for ranking features. The resulting norm-based ranking is subsequently refined through Laplacian-Gated Ranking Correction, which suppresses locally redundant candidates while preserving salient ones. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmark datasets show that ICLFS achieves the best clustering accuracy on 10 datasets against both classical and neural baselines under the standard clustering-based UFS evaluation protocol, while remaining competitive on the other two. These results show that feature-wise contrastive representation consistency provides a strong and effective alternative to neighborhood, cluster, and reconstruction-based UFS formulations.

</details>


### [105] [StudyBench: Can Self-Evolution Squeeze Textbooks for Olympiad Capability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00787)
*Yinghao Chen,Zixi Chen,Bingxiang He,Ziqing Qiao,Huan-ang Gao,Yinuo Xu,Yuxin Zuo,Zeyuan Liu,Yuhao Zhan,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者构建了StudyBench——一个可控物理基准，区分吸收与迁移能力。基准揭示目前自我进化方法在由教材到高难题的能力迁移上存在明显短板（Guidance Gap）并且存在早期的计算饱和（Compute Plateau），表明需要新的方法学突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类比人类通过少量优秀教科书掌握学科并挑战高难问题，期望自我进化方法也能从原始训练材料中自主学习并具备可迁移的问题求解能力。但目前缺乏直接测量这一能力的基准与度量。

Method: 提出StudyBench基准：一个受控的物理问题集，度量自我进化（self-evolution）方法如何将训练材料转化为可迁移的问题求解能力。数据集分为Application Set（教科书难度问题，评估'吸收'能力）和Transfer Set（奥赛级问题，评估'迁移'能力）。对若干代表性自我进化方法在三种基础模型上进行训练与评估，并做引导（guidance）消融与计算量扩展实验以分析瓶颈。

Result: 在Application Set的提升很少能迁移到更难的Transfer Set；存在显著的Guidance Gap：即便最强的方法，在没有把材料作为in‑context引导时，仅能收回极小一部分性能；还观测到Compute Plateau：各方法在远未耗尽计算预算时就饱和。由此可判断差距主要是方法层面的问题，而非数据或算力不足。

Conclusion: StudyBench将自我进化的研究从开放式探索变为可衡量的目标；基准和开源代码为未来攻关Guidance Gap与突破Compute Plateau提供了清晰的评价平台，暗示后续研究应聚焦改进学习algorithms与引导机制以提高从训练材料到可迁移能力的转化效率。

Abstract: Humans need to study only a handful of well-written textbooks to master a discipline and attempt its hardest problems. We argue that an ideal self-evolution method should share the same property, that is autonomously learning from raw training material for transferable problem-solving capability. However, we still lack a direct measurement for it. We introduce StudyBench, a controlled physics benchmark that directly measures how efficiently a self-evolution method converts training material into capability. We organise the test set into an Application Set, consisting of difficult textbook problems and evaluating absorption ability, and a Transfer Set, consisting of olympiad-level problems and evaluating transfer ability. Benchmarking representative self-evolution methods across three base models, we find that improvements on the Application Set rarely translate to the harder Transfer Set. A guidance ablation exposes a Guidance Gap: even the strongest method closes only a small fraction of what the same material unlocks when supplied as in-context guidance. Besides, every method hits a Compute Plateau, saturating well before exhausting its compute budget. The remaining gap is therefore a method problem rather than a data or compute problem. By offering a clean and controlled benchmark, StudyBench turns self-evolution progress from an open-ended pursuit into a measurable target for future research. Our code is released at https://github.com/thunlp/StudyBench.

</details>


### [106] [One Policy, Any Budget: Internalizing Budget-Aware Search via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00813)
*Xiaowei Sun,Jin Li,Yili Hong,Yikun Fu,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnySearch trains a single budget-aware search policy via a scaffolded two-phase curriculum RL and an adaptive composite reward, delivering robust accuracy-efficiency trade-offs and generalization to varied budgets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing RL-trained tool-using LLM agents are trained under fixed budgets and fail to adapt when constraints change at deployment; need a single policy that is budget-aware across a range of constraints.

Method: Two-phase curriculum RL: Phase 1 trains with explicit budget-state injection and structured reasoning prompts under linearly decaying budgets (scaffolded); Phase 2 removes the scaffold and continues RL with adaptively sampled budgets to match inference, using a composite reward combining absolute accuracy and relative efficiency with an adaptive weight.

Result: Outperforms baselines across seven general and multi-hop QA benchmarks over all budget scales, generalizes beyond training-range constraints, and improves tool productivity without excessive token overhead. Code released.

Conclusion: AnySearch enables a single LLM-based search agent to adapt to varying budget constraints at deployment, achieving better accuracy-efficiency trade-offs and generalization to unseen budgets compared to baselines.

Abstract: While reinforcement learning has enabled LLM-based search agents to invoke external tools, existing methods train under fixed budgets and cannot adapt when constraints vary at deployment. We propose AnySearch, a framework that enables a single policy to perform budget-aware search under any budget constraint through a training scaffold and curriculum reinforcement learning. In the first phase, we train the agent with explicit budget state injection and structured reasoning prompts that guide efficient allocation under linearly decaying budgets. In the second phase, the scaffold is removed and the agent learns to operate autonomously under adaptively sampled budget constraints, matching inference conditions. Both phases are optimized with a composite reward that couples answer accuracy with budget efficiency through absolute and relative signals, where an adaptive weight amplifies the efficiency signal for high-accuracy queries and attenuates it for low-accuracy ones. Extensive experiments on seven general and multi-hop QA benchmarks show that our method outperforms baselines across all budget scales, generalizes to unseen constraints beyond the training range, and achieves superior tool productivity without excessive token overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/xwsun01/AnySearch.

</details>


### [107] [AnalysisBank: An Expert Analysis Pattern Library for Financial Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00818)
*Yajing Yang,Yunshan Ma,Kelvin J. L. Koa,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnalysisBank builds a reusable library of fine-grained Analyses (signal+move+span) from expert reports and retrieves+applies these during generation, producing substantially more novel, data-grounded insights than structural baselines and showing transfer beyond finance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Structural/topic/section-level report generation tends to reuse high-level templates and fails to compose novel, data-derived insights. Operating at an analytical granularity promises reusable, data-grounded content that reflects actual expert reasoning.

Method: Distill 550 expert financial reports into a library of ‘Analyses’ (each = data signal + analytical move + expert span); characterize the distribution of signals and moves; at inference, match input signals to library entries and apply retrieved analytical moves to generate report content; evaluate on two financial benchmarks across four LLM backbones and test transfer to scientific writing.

Result: From the distilled Analyses the authors find a heavy-tailed set of ~47–52 signal types and 13 move types. On two benchmarks and four LLMs, AnalysisBank increases the share of novel, data-grounded insights by 1.7–3.7× versus structural baselines; code and library released.

Conclusion: AnalysisBank demonstrates that composing financial reports at an analytical level—by reusing distilled Analyses that pair data signals with analytical moves and expert text spans—yields more novel, data-grounded insights than structural/template-based approaches, and the idea generalizes beyond finance.

Abstract: We argue that financial report generation should operate at the analytical rather than structural level, composing content from data-derived insights rather than high-level topics or sections. To this end, we propose AnalysisBank, which distills expert reports into a reusable library of Analyses, each pairing a data signal, an analytical move, and the expert span it was derived from. At inference time, AnalysisBank matches input signals to library entries and applies the retrieved moves to compose the report. A study of Analyses distilled from 550 expert reports reveals a heavy-tailed distribution of 47-52 signal types spanning 13 move types. On two financial benchmarks across four LLM backbones, AnalysisBank increases the proportion of novel, data-grounded insights by 1.7-3.7x over structural-level baselines. Transfer to scientific writing suggests that the distinction generalizes beyond finance. Code and the distilled Analysis library are available at https://github.com/yajingyang/AnalysisBank.

</details>


### [108] [Polished but Unresolved: Identifying Late-Stage Pressure States in Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00823)
*Haoyang Chen,Yi Liu,Jianzhi Shao,Xiaozhou Xu,Zhe Sun,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Detectable internal 'pressure' causes premature finalization; linear probes and activation edits can mitigate it; PSPR plugin adaptively reduces pressure and improves long-horizon tool-use agents.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agents tend to prematurely finalize answers when solutions look polished but constraints remain unresolved; detecting and mitigating this improves long-horizon tool-use performance.

Method: Train a linear probe to detect pressure direction in hidden states; perform activation interventions to modulate pressure; controlled context manipulations to test causes; design PSPR plugin that applies lightweight relief or structured organization based on pressure level.

Result: Pressure is detectable in hidden states and causal to early submission; interventions change behavior; PSPR consistently strengthens multiple agent baselines on long-horizon benchmarks.

Conclusion: The paper identifies and mitigates a ‘late-stage pressure’ internal state in long-horizon tool-use agents, proposing PSPR which uses probes and activation interventions to relieve pressure and improve agent performance.

Abstract: Long-horizon tool-use agents need not only to search and plan, but also to decide when to finalize. We study late-stage pressure states, in which an agent is biased toward submitting a final answer that appears complete and polished while key constraints remain unresolved. We first train a linear probe to show that this pressure state is identifiable from the agent's hidden states. Then, we use activation interventions along this pressure direction and find that shifting the hidden states changes both the pressure score and whether the agent continues tool use or submits early. Through controlled context manipulations, we further see that the pressure is mitigated by constraint clarity and action mapping. Based on these findings, we propose Probe-Sensed Pressure Relief (PSPR), a plugin that applies lightweight pressure relief direction under moderate pressure and moves to structured organization under high pressure risk. Experiments on multiple long-horizon benchmarks show that our method consistently strengthens existing agent methods.

</details>


### [109] [FLaG: Frequency-Domain Latent-attention Gated Pooling for Token Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00831)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Xueli Wang,Xiwen Gong,Zhongjian Wang,Qiuchen Zhao,Lan Huang,Ruochi Zhang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLaG通过在傅里叶域上使用潜在注意与通道门对编码器输出进行调制与重构，作为可插拔聚合模块在蛋白质、图像和文本任务中普遍带来性能提升，尽管效果依赖骨干与任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有池化多在原始令牌空间操作，可能忽略频域中的全局、冗余与尺度信息。将表示映射到频域并进行学习型汇总可利用频谱结构（低频/高频分布）作为新的归纳偏置，提高聚合稳健性与泛化。

Method: 对编码器输出执行rFFT并将非冗余谱以实部/虚部拼接表示；用可学习的潜在查询对谱令牌做跨注意力（latent cross-attention）进行汇总；生成样本条件的通道门（residual gate）以放大或抑制频谱通道；将调制后的谱重构为令牌表征并进行下游聚合（池化）。该模块为插入式，可用于ESM2（AMP预测）、ResNet18（CIFAR-10/100）和RoBERTa（若干语言任务）。

Result: 在四个AMP骨干-物种设置上，FLaG取得宏观Spearman相关系数、RMSE和Recall@50的最佳平均值；在CIFAR-10上得到最高top-1准确率；在七个语言度量中5项平均表现最佳（但STSB仍由mean pooling领先）。机制分析显示：大多数层对低频敏感，最终层高频相对敏感性上升；残差门普遍放大谱通道但保留低频主能量；潜在交叉注意表现出样本与物种特异的频谱分配。

Conclusion: FLaG提出了一种可插拔的频域池化模块，通过在傅里叶域上对编码器输出进行重表达、用可学习的潜在查询汇总谱令牌并通过样本条件通道门调制，提升了多模态（蛋白质、图像、文本）表示聚合的效果。总体结论是：频域聚合提供了一种跨任务、跨骨干网的有用归纳偏置，但其收益与骨干网络和下游任务相关。

Abstract: Token aggregation converts token-level representations into fixed-dimensional sample representations, but most pooling methods operate only in the original token space. We introduce Frequency-Domain Latent-attention Gated Pooling (FLaG), a plug-in aggregation module that re-expresses encoder outputs in the Fourier domain before final pooling. FLaG represents the nonredundant rFFT spectrum through concatenated real and imaginary components, summarizes spectral tokens with learnable latent queries, derives a sample-conditioned channel gate, and reconstructs modulated token representations for downstream aggregation. We evaluate the same architecture across ESM2-based antimicrobial peptide (AMP) activity prediction, ResNet18 image classification on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, and three RoBERTa-based language tasks. FLaG achieves the best macro-averaged Spearman correlation coefficient, RMSE, and Recall@50 across four AMP backbone-species settings and the highest top-1 accuracy on CIFAR 10. It also achieves the best mean results on five of seven language metrics, although mean pooling remains strongest on STSBenchmark. AMP-side mechanistic analyses reveal low-frequency prediction sensitivity across most encoder layers, with increased relative high-frequency sensitivity in the final layer, and pronounced peptide-specific positional responses. The residual gate broadly amplifies spectral channels while preserving the low-frequency-dominated energy profile, whereas latent cross-attention exhibits sample- and species-specific spectral allocation. Overall, FLaG provides a transferable frequency-domain aggregation bias across protein, visual, and textual representations, with benefits that depend on the backbone and downstream task. Supplementary materials, source code, and data are available at https://www.healthinformaticslab.org/supp/ and https://github.com/Kewei2023/AMPCliff/tree/FLaG.

</details>


### [110] [Towards Generalizable Visually Grounded Exploration of Household Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00845)
*Linhao Zheng,Zeming Liu,Wangke Chen,Li Zeng,Wanxiang Che,Heyan Huang,Yuhang Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VGEBench基准与逻辑驱动状态机模拟多回合交互，用以评估VLM在无手册条件下将抽象知识主动落地为视觉可操作行为的能力；实验表明现有模型存在显著短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的具身探索范式过度依赖人工标注轨迹和显式文档，限制了智能体在未见设备或无手册场景下的泛化能力；需要一个能测试将抽象世界知识落地为细粒度视觉可操作性（affordances）的评测基准。

Method: 构建基于逻辑驱动的状态机（Logic-Driven State Machine）框架，以模拟多回合的假设-交互-修正（Hypothesis-Interaction-Refinement）循环，迫使智能体通过主动视觉感知与反馈驱动的纠正来实现目标，而非依赖静态文档或标注轨迹。

Result: 基准实验显示，现有VLM在跨设备泛化、将高层语义映射到低层可执行动作以及在长时序交互中维持精确状态估计方面表现不佳，证明了VGEBench对检验通用视觉落地探索能力的必要性。

Conclusion: 本文提出VGEBench基准以评估视觉-语言模型在通用视觉落地式探索任务上的表现，结论是现有VLM在将语义知识转化为物理执行与维持长时序状态跟踪方面存在显著挑战。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in static visual recognition and high-level semantic reasoning. However, current embodied exploration paradigms still heavily rely on imitation learning from human-annotated trajectories, which severely limits agents' generalization ability. The key bottleneck of realizing general autonomous embodied agents lies in Generalizable Visually Grounded Exploration: the ability to operate novel devices without manuals or specific training by actively grounding abstract world knowledge into fine-grained visual affordances. Yet, existing benchmarks fail to evaluate this capability: they generally rely on explicit documents and annotated trajectories, neglecting the dynamic Hypothesis-Interaction-Refinement process essential for functional device operation. To bridge this gap, we introduce VGEBench, a comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate the generalizable visually grounded exploration capabilities of VLMs. Unlike static datasets, we construct a Logic-Driven State Machine framework. This framework simulates multi-turn interaction loops, compelling agents to achieve goals by active visual perception and feedback-driven correction. Experimental results demonstrate that existing VLMs face significant challenges in translating semantic knowledge into physical execution and maintaining long-horizon state tracking.

</details>


### [111] [Verifiable Disaster Storylines and Causal Knowledge Graphs: A Citation-Grounded Pipeline from Heterogeneous Humanitarian Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00858)
*Ivan Decostanzi,Michele Ronco,Sergio Consoli,Christina Corbane,Lorenzo Bertolini,Indaco Biazzo,Daria Mihaila,Manuel Garcia-Herranz,Felix Schwebel,Yelena Mejova,Kyriaki Kalimeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于 RAG 的可追溯灾害情报流水线，将 EM-DAT 与 ReliefWeb/EMM 融合，产出结构化事件概要与带引用的因果知识图谱；小规模人类评估结果有力但尚需更全面的量化与鲁棒性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在危机早期需要快速综合大量异构信息，人工分析跟不上节奏，因而需自动化、可追溯的情报合成工具来提升响应效率与准确性。

Method: 采用 Retrieval-Augmented Generation（RAG）框架：检索异构源（EM-DAT、ReliefWeb、EMM），把检索到的证据用于生成17字段的结构化事件概要（表格化 profile）并抽取因果关系，构建节点/边均以引用文本丰富的因果知识图谱。

Result: 在三个危机用例和 9 名领域专家 + 9 名非专家的人类评估中，系统展示了高检索精度、因果关系抽取的较强可信度，且专家更偏好带引用的组件；系统设计具备扩展至完整 EM-DAT 目录的目标。

Conclusion: 提出的流水线成功将结构化的 EM-DAT 记录与 ReliefWeb、EMM 的非结构化文档结合，生成了带来源可追溯的灾害叙事和因果知识图谱，能够支持应急情报的可追溯决策。

Abstract: Effective humanitarian response depends on the rapid synthesis of heterogeneous, high-volume information sources - a task that routinely exceeds human analytical capacity in the critical early hours of a crisis. We present a pipeline that combines structured disaster records from EM-DAT with unstructured documents from ReliefWeb and the European Media Monitor (EMM) to produce source-grounded disaster storylines and causal knowledge graphs supporting situational awareness for responders and analysts. Using Retrieval-Augmented Generation, the pipeline extracts structured storylines - tabular event profiles covering 17 fields, from severity and key drivers to child-sensitive impact indicators - and constructs causal knowledge graphs where each node and edge is enriched with citation-grounded explanatory narratives, enabling full traceability back to primary sources. We evaluate the system on three diverse crisis use cases through a human evaluation involving 9 domain expert and 9 non-expert evaluators. Results confirm high retrieval precision, strong faithfulness of extracted causal relations, and a clear expert preference for citation-grounded components over ungrounded alternatives. The pipeline is designed to scale to the full EM-DAT catalogue, with the goal of publicly releasing a narrative-enriched version of the database.

</details>


### [112] [Reinforcement Learning Enhanced LLM Agents for Complex Vehicle Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00859)
*Yi Chen,Zikang Yu,Jiahai Wang,Jinbiao Chen,Jianpeng Zhou,Zizhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RLEA通过软Q学习驱动的Planner与基于LLM的多智能体协同，以及进化记忆与RAG，能更可靠地自动生成求解器模型，显著提高复杂VRP自动建模的成功率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管高阶优化求解器能有效解决VRP，但将复杂VRP变体正确建模到求解器中通常需要大量领域知识，阻碍了先进优化技术的广泛可用性。

Method: 提出RLEA（Reinforcement Learning Enhanced LLMAgents）多智能体框架：引入用软Q学习训练的轻量级神经Planner来协调LLM驱动的代理行动，并结合进化记忆模块与检索增强生成（RAG）以在程序生成与迭代优化过程中利用历史经验与外部求解器知识。

Result: 在48种不同的VRP变体和多种求解器上评估，RLEA较之前最先进方法成功率提高了16.67%，并显著减少了运行时错误。

Conclusion: 将强化学习与大语言模型推理相结合能显著提升自动化建模复杂车辆路径问题的能力，降低对领域专家的依赖。

Abstract: Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are fundamental combinatorial optimization problems with widespread applications in various scenarios. The advanced optimization solvers can effectively solve such problems. However, modeling complex VRP variants for solvers often requires substantial domain expertise, which limits the accessibility of advanced optimization technologies. In this paper, we propose Reinforcement Learning Enhanced LLMAgents(RLEA), a multi-agent framework designed to automate the modeling of complex VRPs. RLEA introduces a lightweight neural Planner trained with Soft Q-learning to efficiently orchestrate the actions of LLM-based agents. In addition, we equip the system with an evolutionary memory module and retrieval-augmented generation, enabling the agent to leverage both accumulated experience and external solver knowledge during program generation and refinement for solving VRPs. We evaluated 48 distinct VRP variants across various solvers. The experimental results demonstrate that RLEA outperforms the previous state-of-the-ar method, achieving a 16.67% higher success rate while significantly reducing runtime errors. These results validate that integrating reinforcement learning with LLM-based reasoning is highly effective for automated optimization modeling. The appendix is available at: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19134435.

</details>


### [113] [Beyond the Clock: Measuring the Value of Adaptive Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00874)
*Ayushi Chadha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在这类分层系统中，学习到的元层修正时机虽会依赖内部状态并改变行为，但并不保证超过好的固定时序基线；因此评估元层控制应分别考察状态依赖、行为改变和超越强非自适应策略的结果价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当智能体成为复合系统时，重要决策上升到任务执行之上：管理器需要决定何时保持已有策略、何时修改它。作者旨在探究学习到的元层控制是否真的带来比强非自适应基线更高的任务价值，以及状态依赖性如何与实际性能关联。

Method: 构建并训练一个分层潜在推理器（hierarchical latent reasoner），其管理器可选择保留（PERSIST）或替换（REPLAN）下层计算的承诺。使用三个预提交（precommitted）训练随机种子得到学习到的时机策略；在冻结检查点上，将学习到的策略与一组强制（forced）时序策略比较。进一步通过干预研究（intervention）、穷举固定时序（exhaustive enumeration）以及反事实PERSIST/REPLAN诊断来分析时机与可预测性的来源。

Result: 主要发现包括：不同训练种子产生的修正时机策略在行为上差异很大（从接近确定的早期时钟到高度状态条件化的分布）；但在同一冻结检查点上，学习到的策略没有超过最佳固定时序策略。时机本身对结果有实质影响且顺序敏感；穷举表明一个强固定时序已捕捉了大部分可测量价值；反事实诊断显示，当可预测性主要由决策位置（position）而非位置内判别驱动时，得分证据可能具有误导性。

Conclusion: 作者结论是：在他们研究的分层潜在推理器中，学习到的元层修改（revision）时机虽然在行为上呈现出基于状态的多样性，但并未在冻结的检查点上超过最好的固定时序策略；因此仅有状态依赖并不必然带来可复制的任务性能提升。需要把元层控制的评估分成三轴：分数是否依赖状态、这种依赖是否改变实际行为、以及这些行为改变是否能在强非自适应基线之外带来结果价值。

Abstract: As agentic systems become compound systems, increasingly important decisions move above task execution itself: when should a higher-level controller preserve the strategy guiding another process, and when should it revise it? We study this meta-level control problem in a hierarchical latent reasoner whose manager can retain or replace a commitment governing lower-level computation. Across three precommitted training seeds, learned revision timing produces qualitatively different policies, ranging from an almost deterministic early clock to substantially more state conditioned schedule distributions, yet none outperforms the best forced timing policy evaluated on the same frozen checkpoint. This separates state dependence from decision value: a controller can vary its actions with internal state without turning that variation into a reproducible task-performance benefit. A deeper intervention study on the original checkpoint shows that timing itself is consequential and order-sensitive, while exhaustive enumeration reveals that a strong fixed schedule captures most of the measurable value available from timing at this decision budget. Counterfactual PERSIST/REPLAN diagnostics further show why score-level evidence can be misleading when predictability is dominated by decision position rather than within-position discrimination. Together, these results argue that learned meta-level control should be evaluated along three separate axes: whether its score depends on state, whether that dependence changes realized behavior, and whether those changes capture outcome value beyond a strong non-adaptive policy.

</details>


### [114] [FractalNet-Based Heterogeneous Federated Learning for Orbital Edge Intelligence in Satellite Mega-Constellations: A Wildfire Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00875)
*Sai Puppala,Koushik Sinha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对卫星巨星座的资源与链路异质性，提出基于FractalNet的分层深度异构联邦学习及路径调度与周期性分层聚合，仿真结果表明在效率与鲁棒性上具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面中心化或地面继承的联邦学习架构无法满足卫星在体积、能耗、辐射耐受、链路可用性和传播延迟方面的量级差异；需要异构、分层和时变可调的学习系统。

Method: 提出深度异质联邦优化问题并引入分布式路径调度器，根据卫星SWAP‑C约束、预测接触窗口和训练统计分配模型深度；采用分层周期性聚合（按层汇聚而非每次接触），并设计三层代理控制面负责调度、异常上报和策略自治。以火灾监测为案例，低轨/中轨/高轨分别学习像素级、区域级和大尺度语义。

Result: 在模拟的巨星座场景中，方法在收敛速度、通信开销、能耗自适应、定期汇聚带来的开销减少、鲁棒性与端到端延迟等指标上均显示出优于常规模型的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作提出并验证了一种面向卫星巨星座的深度异质化联邦学习框架，基于FractalNet架构与分布式路径调度，可在受限SWAP‑C、时变链路和延迟环境中改善收敛性、通信效率与能耗适配。

Abstract: Satellite mega-constellations are emerging as large-scale sensing, communication, and computation fabrics, yet their learning architectures remain largely inherited from terrestrial federated learning and ground-centric mission operations--- ill-suited to satellites that differ by orders of magnitude in Size, Weight, Power, and Cost (SWAP-C), radiation tolerance, link availability, and propagation delay. We propose a heterogeneous federated learning method based on the FractalNet architecture for orbital edge intelligence. We formalize contact-window-constrained, depth-heterogeneous federated optimization and introduce a distributed path scheduler that assigns model depth as a function of SWAP-C constraints, predicted inter-satellite contacts, and training statistics. To reduce message overhead and energy consumption, each tier pools updates periodically rather than at every contact opportunity, and a three-tier agentic control plane governs in-space scheduling, anomaly escalation, and policy-governed autonomy. As a case study, we apply the framework to wildfire detection, where each orbital shell naturally learns a different semantic level of situational awareness: pixel-scale thermal anomalies at low Earth orbit (LEO), regional fire-front dynamics at medium Earth orbit (MEO), and larger-scale risk propagation at geostationary or high Earth orbit (GEO/HEO). Experiments on simulated mega-constellations validate the approach across convergence, communication efficiency, energy adaptation, scheduled-pooling savings, robustness, and latency.

</details>


### [115] [Towards reliable multimodal disaster severity assessment through preference optimization and explainable vision-language reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00879)
*Yuanjun Zhang,Fuzel Ahamed Shaik,Suvojit Acharjee,Fahad Khalid,Mourad Oussalah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者通过一个从单一 HITL 构建出 ReasoningSet 与 PreferenceSet 的统一流水线，先用 SFT 提升分类与解释基础能力，再用 DPO 做偏好对齐，达成更可靠且可审计的多模态灾害损伤评估系统，并在两种模型上验证了效果与通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态灾害损伤评估受限于标注数据稀缺且对模型推理质量评估不足，需要既准确又可审计的可解释模型以支持应急决策

Method: 基于单一 HITL 流程构建两个数据集：ReasoningSet（带验证的推理/理由）用于监督微调（SFT），和 PreferenceSet（成对理由）用于基于 Direct Preference Optimization（DPO）的偏好对齐；用自动指标、模型打分与人工排序联合评估分类性能与解释质量，并在两个基础大模型（InternVL-3-8B与LLaVA-1.5-7B）上进行交叉验证

Result: SFT 将准确率从73.64%提升到78.29%，Macro-F1 提升约29%，解释质量提升约25%；在完成 DPO 对齐后，解释可解释性与与人工判断的一致性进一步加强；对轻微损伤样本检测更敏感，降低高风险错误分类；在 InternVL 与 LLaVA 上展示了鲁棒性与可迁移性

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段训练框架（SFT + DPO）在灾害多模态损伤评估任务上同时提升了分类准确性与生成性解释的质量，并通过单一 HITL 注释流程高效构建了用于监督微调与偏好对齐的互补数据集

Abstract: Reliable disaster damage assessment requires models that provide both accurate predictions and transparent explanations. However, existing multimodal approaches are limited by scarce annotated data and insufficient evaluation of reasoning quality. This study proposes a two-stage training framework that integrates Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) within a unified data construction pipeline. From a single Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) annotation workflow, two complementary datasets are derived, namely ReasoningSet, which contains validated rationales for SFT, and PreferenceSet, which comprises paired rationales for DPO-based alignment. The framework evaluates both classification performance and explanation quality using automatic metrics, model-based scoring, and human ranking. Experimental results show that SFT improves accuracy from 73.64% to 78.29% and increases Macro-F1 by 29% compared to the baseline, while explanation quality improves by approximately 25%. Subsequent DPO alignment further enhances interpretability on the PreferenceSet. Cross-model validation on InternVL-3-8B and LLaVA-1.5-7B demonstrates the robustness and generalizability of the approach. The proposed framework improves detection of underrepresented mild damage cases, reduces high-risk misclassifications, and strengthens alignment between model reasoning and human judgment. Overall, it provides a reproducible pathway to develop reliable multimodal systems that deliver auditable, actionable disaster insights for emergency management.

</details>


### [116] [Denoising Diffusion Generative Models Secretly Calculate Attentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00885)
*Farzan Haddadi,Leila Monfared,Ebrahim Rezaii,Mohammadreza Malek-Mohammadi,Pejman Zakalvand,Narges Mokhtari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 证明扩散模型可被视为含注意力机制的等价表示，并据此提出一种计算更省的注意力驱动图像生成算法，性能接近传统扩散方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合图像生成（扩散模型）与自然语言生成（Transformer/注意力）之间的架构差异，寻找统一原理并降低扩散模型训练与采样的高昂计算成本。

Method: 理论推导与架构等价性分析：证明扩散过程内隐地实现了与Transformer相近的注意力操作；比较自编码器与注意力模型的功能原理并建立映射；基于推导提出一种基于注意力的简化生成算法并在图像生成任务上实现与扩散模型的对比实验。

Result: 提出的注意力实现的生成算法在多项图像生成任务上达到与标准扩散模型可比的质量指标，但训练与推理耗时、显存或计算量显著降低（论文宣称“显著更少的计算资源”）。

Conclusion: 扩散模型和基于注意力的Transformer在本质上共享类似的注意力机制，注意力可以被视为一种普适的机器学习原则；基于该等价性，可将扩散框架重构为基于注意力的简化算法，从而显著降低训练与生成的计算负担，同时在性能上可达到可比水平。

Abstract: Denoising diffusion models are the dominant architecture for image generation, whereas most natural language generation and modeling are primarily handled by well-known transformer architectures employing attention mechanism. Here, we show that diffusion models also inherently use an attention mechanism very similar to that of transformers. Therefore, attention emerges as a universal machine learning principle, based on a general training objective. We also show similarities in basic functional principle of auto-encoders and attention-based models. These equivalences allows us to interchange these designs based on practical requirements. As an example, we can reformulate the diffusion framework to reduce the lengthy training process and computation-intensive image generation. Using this approach, a simplified algorithm is proposed for image generation which is based on attention mechanism. Results show that the attention-based implementation achieves comparable performance with significantly less effort and computational resources.

</details>


### [117] [CacheBridge: Efficient Cross-Model KV Cache Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00891)
*Xingyu Qu,Siyuan Lu,Zhiyu Chen,Sheng Wang,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CacheBridge 通过一对一头映射、注意力加权校准与融合 GPU 统计构造，在保持闭式映射接口的同时，大幅降低存储与延迟并提高跨模型 KV 迁移的鲁棒性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Full-Head Mapping 将每个目标头从所有源头映射，随着层数与架构差异增加会导致迁移质量下降、映射体积与应用开销急剧增长。需要一种低成本、训练免费且对架构差异更鲁棒的跨模型 KV 传递方法。

Method: 提出三段协同设计：1) 架构索引的映射支持——将每个目标 KV 头限制到一个匹配的源头以减少过拟合与冗余；2) 注意力对齐校准——使用因果注意力敏感度对重建误差加权以优先保留重要上下文影响；3) 有界映射构造与高效实现——采用融合 GPU 内核在不显式构造观测张量的情况下计算加权充分统计量，保留闭式仿射解以便在线部署。

Result: 在三个迁移方向上，CacheBridge 恢复了 Full-Head Mapping 在两条 Ministral 3 路径上丢失的精度；在 Qwen3 上实现了 99.83% 的平均目标保持率。Qwen3 从 14B 到 32B 的迁移中，映射存储量减少 8×，应用速度最高提升 3.0×，校准数据需求降至 Full-Head 的十分之一，500 序列的构造时间从 92.63s 降到 8.63s（10.7×）。

Conclusion: CacheBridge 在保持训练免费、闭式仿射映射接口的同时，通过限制头到头的一一映射、按因果注意力敏感度加权校准和有界的映射构造，显著降低了映射存储与应用成本，并在多条迁移路径上恢复或超越了 Full-Head Mapping 的性能。

Abstract: Sharing context between LLMs in a multi-model system requires the receiving model to prefill the shared prefix because KV caches are model-specific. Recent closed-form cross-model KV transfer, hereafter Full-Head Mapping, avoids this replay by fitting a training-free affine mapper from source to target caches. However, its full-head design maps each target KV head from every source KV head in the selected layers, making transfer quality sensitive to architectural differences and causing mapper storage and application cost to grow with layer support. To this end, we introduce CacheBridge, which co-designs architecture-indexed mapper support, attention-aligned calibration, and bounded mapper construction while retaining a closed-form affine interface for online deployment. CacheBridge restricts each target head to a matched source head, weights reconstruction errors by causal attention sensitivity, and uses a fused GPU kernel to construct weighted sufficient statistics without materializing full observation tensors. Across three transfer directions, CacheBridge recovers the two Ministral 3 transfer directions where Full-Head Mapping loses substantial accuracy while preserving 99.83\% mean target retention on Qwen3. On Qwen3 $14\mathrm{B}\to32\mathrm{B}$, it reduces mapper storage by $8\times$, accelerates application by up to $3.0\times$, matches \fullhead with one tenth of the calibration data, and reduces 500-sequence construction from 92.63 to 8.63 seconds ($10.7\times$).

</details>


### [118] [CARE: Contrastive Anchor-based Rubric Evolution for Large Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00892)
*Siyuan Li,Xinxin Song,Chen Ruinian,Jingjing Fan,Tingxiong Xiao,Yangen Hu,Ke Zeng,Jinli Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARE uses contrastive comparisons to a frontier-model anchor to guide dynamic rubric evolution via Adaptive and Chase branches, preventing rubric hacking and improving policy alignment; empirical results show SOTA performance and sustained gains against strong anchors.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Static rubrics are vulnerable to being hacked as policies improve; prior dynamic rubric methods suffer from undirected extraction, unreliable hack detection, and uncontrolled rubric proliferation. CARE aims to address these failure modes by anchoring rubric updates to high-quality model outputs.

Method: Contrastive Anchor-based Rubric Evolution (CARE): at each training step generate a high-quality anchor response from a frontier model conditioned on prompt and rubrics; contrast the highest-scoring rollout with the anchor, and apply two branches—Adaptive (reactive repair of reward misspecification) and Chase (proactive conversion of frontier-level quality gaps into sharper rubrics)—to evolve rubrics.

Result: On WildChecklist-9K with Qwen2.5-7B-Base and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, CARE attains SOTA on Arena-Hard-2.0, InfoBench, and FollowBench. It is the only method showing sustained win-rate improvement against GPT-4.1 anchor responses over 300 training steps. Additional experiments on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen3-8B indicate cross-family generalization.

Conclusion: CARE maintains discriminative accuracy in the high-reward region, improving robustness of rubric-based RL by grounding rubric evolution in high-quality anchor responses; it achieves state-of-the-art empirical results and sustained improvement against strong anchors.

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning decomposes open-ended instructions into prompt-specific, flexible rubrics, making it better suited than reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for post-training LLMs on open-ended tasks. However, static rubrics are inevitably hacked as the policy evolves, and existing dynamic approaches introduce new problems: undirected rubric extraction, unreliable hack detection, and unbounded rubric proliferation. We propose $\textbf{CARE}$ ($\textbf{C}$ontrastive $\textbf{A}$nchor-based $\textbf{R}$ubric $\textbf{E}$volution), which grounds every rubric evolution step in a high-quality anchor response generated by a frontier model conditioned on the prompt and its rubrics. At each training step, CARE contrasts the highest-scoring rollout against the anchor, enabling two complementary mechanisms: an Adaptive branch that reactively repairs reward misspecification; and a Chase branch that proactively converts frontier-level quality gaps into sharper rubrics. Together, the two branches $\textbf{maintain discriminative accuracy in the high-reward region}$---the precise region where reward over-optimization mostly originates. Experiments on WildChecklist-9K with Qwen2.5-7B-Base and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct show that CARE achieves state-of-the-art performance on Arena-Hard-2.0, InfoBench, and FollowBench, and is the $\textbf{only}$ method whose win rate against GPT-4.1 anchor responses shows sustained improvement throughout 300 training steps; additional results on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen3-8B further indicate that CARE generalizes across model families.

</details>


### [119] [In-Context Neurofeedback: Can LLMs Control Their Internal Representations through Privileged Access?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00904)
*Koshiro Aoki,Ryota Takatsuki,Gouki Minegishi,Yusuke Haruki,Daisuke Kawahara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将神经反馈目标设为不可由提示推断的“特权”表征后，LLM未能可靠控制其内部表征，提示需用特权访问评估方法来严格测试模型的元认知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估LLM是否能在内部表征层面进行自我调控，这对机器元认知与AI安全重要；指出先前使用的神经反馈实验可能未能排除表面性策略，因此需要更贴近人类认知神经科学的严格实验设计。

Method: 重新设计了针对LLM的神经反馈范式，使控制目标满足“特权访问”要求（即目标不能被第三方仅从提示推断），并在该更严格的设置下对模型进行训练与测试，比较其在原始与特权访问条件下的表现差异。

Result: 在满足特权访问的严格范式中，模型未表现出对特权内部表征的可靠控制；这表明之前的正面结果不能排除模型仅通过提示可见的信号或其他外部可推断线索实现控制的可能性。

Conclusion: 在更严格的“特权访问”设置下，现有的大语言模型并不能可靠地控制其内部表征；此前报告的“可控性”很可能依赖于表面性机制（如可从提示推断的目标），而非真实的内部访问能力。

Abstract: Whether large language models (LLMs) can control their own internal representations matters for both machine metacognition and AI safety. A recent study applied neurofeedback to LLMs and claimed that they can control their internal representations. However, the reported control may rely on superficial mechanisms rather than genuine internal access because the control targets in that study are not privileged, meaning that a third party can infer them from the prompt. We redesign the neurofeedback paradigm for LLMs so that the control target satisfies the privileged access requirement, which is closer to neurofeedback experiments in human cognitive neuroscience. Under this stricter setting, the models do not demonstrate reliable control over privileged internal representations, suggesting that previously reported control cannot exclude the possibility that it relies on superficial mechanisms. Our results indicate that rigorous assessments of metacognition in LLMs require evaluation methods that demand privileged access.

</details>


### [120] [RPCBench: A Benchmark for Proactive Premise Critique in LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00918)
*Zhongru Chen,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RPCBench，评估大模型在推荐对话中识别/定位/处理错误前提的能力；发现主动检测能力欠缺、欠定前提最难、推理过长反而有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐评估多聚焦排序/生成/偏好满足，而忽视模型在自然语言推荐请求中识别并处理错误前提的能力。需要一个针对推荐场景、证据驱动的错误检测与诊断基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建基于证据的测试集（五个推荐领域、十类前提失败），每个样本包含可见的推荐上下文与被篡改的用户请求；并设计细粒度评价指标：主动检测、错误定位、后处理策略与证据忠实度；用11个大模型进行系统评估以分析表现模式与误区。

Result: 发布RPCBench数据集与评价框架；系统评估显示模型在主动检测上表现最弱，对欠定前提错误最敏感；关键信息密度比冗余证据更影响性能；中等长度推理优于过短或过长推理。

Conclusion: RPCBench提出并验证了用于评估推荐系统中模型识别并处理“错误前提”（faulty premises）能力的基准，发现主动检测是关键瓶颈，欠定前提最难处理，推理过长会导致“过度思考”惩罚。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as interactive recommender assistants. Their evaluation should therefore go beyond plausible item recommendation and test whether they can recognize flawed recommendation requests. Existing recommender benchmarks mainly assess ranking, generation, or preference satisfaction, while existing error-detection benchmarks are usually not grounded in recommendation-specific user and candidate evidence. To address this gap, we introduce RPCBench, a benchmark for evaluating Recommender-Premise Critique: the ability to detect, diagnose, and properly handle faulty premises in natural-language recommendation requests. RPCBench contains evidence-grounded test instances from five recommendation domains and covers ten types of premise failures. Each instance provides a visible recommendation context and a corrupted user query. We further design a fine-grained evaluation framework that measures proactive detection, error localization, post-detection handling strategy, and evidence faithfulness. Through a systematic evaluation of 11 LLMs, we find that proactive detection is the main bottleneck in Recommender-Premise Critique, and models perform worst on underspecified-premise errors. We also observe that target-critical information density matters more than redundant evidence, and that longer reasoning does not monotonically improve critique quality: performance peaks at intermediate reasoning length, while overly long reasoning is accompanied by an overthinking penalty. The code is available at https://github.com/ZhongruChen/RPCBench.

</details>


### [121] [VIBE-Bench: Evaluating Personalized Large Language Models When Profiles Don't Mean Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00921)
*Yiwen Jiang,Yang Deng,Stephanie Fong,Zimu Wang,Yaling Shen,Wei Feng,Hongxi Yang,Xiangyu Zhao,Zhongxing Xu,Deval Mehta,Xuelian Cheng,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VIBE-Bench exposes a key limitation of current personalized LLMs—profile-preference conceptual misalignment—by providing psychology-grounded tasks that require mapping across concept spaces; experiments show existing methods rely on surface semantics and fail, motivating new approaches for true preference reasoning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing personalization benchmarks assume query-relevant preferences can be retrieved via semantic similarity with user history. The study targets the underexplored regime where profile cues and preferences are conceptually misaligned and semantic matching fails, which reflects real-world personalization challenges.

Method: Constructed VIBE-Bench, a benchmark with two psychology-grounded tasks, 3,504 personas and 12,239 dialogues (including a manually verified gold test set) designed to require cross-concept preference reasoning; evaluated multiple existing personalization methods on this benchmark to measure robustness beyond surface semantic overlap.

Result: Empirical results show current PLLMs largely exploit shallow semantic correlations and fail to learn robust cross-concept mappings. VIBE-Bench surfaces this failure regime and provides a focused testbed for developing methods that perform preference reasoning beyond semantic matching.

Conclusion: Profile-preference conceptual misalignment (PRCM) is a distinct and practically important failure mode for personalized LLMs: when observable profile cues and query-specific preferences live in different concept spaces, semantic retrieval-based personalization breaks down.

Abstract: Personalized Large Language Models (PLLMs) aim to tailor responses to individual users, where a central challenge is preference reasoning: inferring query-relevant preferences from user-related history. Existing benchmarks, however, largely assume that such preference can be retrieved from semantically related history. We study an underexplored but practically important regime, profile-preference conceptual misalignment (PRCM), where observable profile cues and query-specific preferences lie in different concept spaces, making semantic retrieval inconsistent for personalization. We introduce VIBE-Bench, a benchmark with two psychology-grounded tasks, 3,504 personas and 12,239 dialogues, including a manually verified gold test set, and requires cross-concept preference reasoning beyond surface semantic overlap. Experiments with several personalization methods show that current PLLMs largely rely on shallow semantic correlations and fail to acquire robust cross-concept mappings. These findings establish PRCM as a distinct failure regime in PLLMs and position VIBE-Bench as a focused testbed for advancing preference reasoning beyond semantic matching.

</details>


### [122] [Few-Shot Out of Domain Intent Detection with Covariance Corrected Mahalanobis Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00961)
*Jayasimha Talur,Oleg Smirnov,Paul Missault*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对少样本OOD意图检测，本文通过对类内协方差进行校正并应用于马氏距离计算，有效改善了先前方法在小样本下的性能缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话代理需识别未见过的意图（OOD），而先前基于马氏距离的方法在少样本场景表现不佳，需要改进以满足实际对话系统的少样本学习需求。

Method: 分析了Podolskiy等人（2021）原始马氏距离方法在少样本设置下的缺陷，提出对类内协方差矩阵进行校正（显式或正则化处理），并将校正后的协方差用于计算马氏距离来判别OOD样本。

Result: 校正后的马氏距离在少样本实验中显著提升了OOD检测性能，超过原始马氏方法和若干基线方法，证明协方差校正对稳定性和判别力有正面影响。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种协方差校正的马氏距离方法，用于改进少样本情形下的域外（OOD）意图检测，解决了先前方法在小样本下性能下降的问题。

Abstract: Conversational agents like chatbots and voice assistants are trained to understand and respond to user intents. On encountering an utterance with an intent different from the ones they have been trained on, these agents are expected to classify the intent as `unknown' or `out of domain'. This problem is known as out of domain (OOD) intent detection. Podolskiy et al. (2021), showed that Mahalanobis distance can be used effectively for identifying OOD intents, outperforming competing approaches. However, their method fails to outperform the baselines in the practically important few-shot setting. In this paper we analyze the reason for low performance and propose a covariance corrected Mahalanobis distance for detecting out-of-domain intents.

</details>


### [123] [CoBRA: Learning Tool-Use Boundaries via Counterfactual Margins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00967)
*Wenhao Zou,Xianglong Liu,Wendong Bi,Hanjie Wang,Simin Zhao,Gong Zhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoBRA learns an explicit counterfactual margin between answering with vs without tools, uses it to train boundary-aware policies (SFT + RL), and yields better, more efficient tool-calling decisions for retrieval-augmented LLMs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Absolute signals (difficulty, confidence, final reward) fail to capture instance-level marginal benefit of tool use; an explicit counterfactual estimate of the marginal utility of tools can guide when to call them to reduce cost/latency/noise while preserving answer quality for knowledge-intensive queries.

Method: Construct internal and external experts from the same base model, collect paired trajectories, estimate reward margins between using and not using tools, partition instances into internal-favored/external-favored/ambiguous, perform Boundary-Aware Cold-Start SFT on clear-margin samples, then apply MARS-RL with reference-split rollouts and counterfactual marginal advantages to optimize boundary decisions.

Result: On retrieval-augmented Qwen3-4B experiments, CoBRA increases tool-use efficiency, improves boundary-sensitive answer accuracy, and maintains strong performance on out-of-distribution, tool-dependent questions.

Conclusion: CoBRA effectively learns when to use external tools by estimating instance-level counterfactual reward margins, improving tool-use efficiency and preserving performance on tool-dependent tasks.

Abstract: As large language models increasingly act through external tools, deciding when to call a tool has become a central problem alongside deciding how to use it. Unnecessary tool calls introduce latency, cost, retrieval noise, and error propagation, while missed calls hurt knowledge-intensive queries or questions requiring up-to-date evidence. Existing methods typically trigger tools from absolute query or generation signals, such as difficulty, confidence, or final task reward, and therefore lack an explicit estimate of the instance-level marginal benefit of tool use. We propose CoBRA, a counterfactual boundary-learning framework for tool-augmented language models. CoBRA first constructs internal and external experts from the same base model, collects paired trajectories, and estimates the reward margin between answering with and without tools. This margin partitions data into internal-favored, external-favored, and ambiguous cases. CoBRA then uses clear-margin samples for Boundary-Aware Cold-Start SFT, followed by MARS-RL with reference-split rollouts and counterfactual marginal advantages to optimize boundary decisions. Experiments with retrieval as the main tool on Qwen3-4B show that CoBRA improves tool-use efficiency and boundary-sensitive answer accuracy while maintaining strong performance on tool-dependent out-of-distribution questions.

</details>


### [124] [Figures as Programs: Recursive Generation of Editable Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01006)
*Yepeng Liu,Dasen Dai,Chengzhi Liu,Yiren Song,Hai Ci,Yu Zhang,Qi Zhang,Mike Zheng Shou,Xin Eric Wang,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 
提出 FigTree：通过递归 SVG 程序构建与渲染-批评器循环，将论文自动转为高质量、可编辑的矢量科研图像，优于传统位图生成方法。



<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 
科研方法图在传达复杂方法时必不可少，但手工制作耗时且常需反复修订。现有图像生成模型虽能合成栩栩如生的位图，但单步生成很难达到人类满意程度，且对位图进行精确编辑困难。因此提出基于可编辑矢量程序的自动化生成方案以提升质量与可编辑性。


Method: 
FigTree 先将图内容与论文文本对齐（grounding），将整个图分解为层次化的局部区域，每个区域由短小的 SVG 程序生成，再将片段拼装为完整图像；并引入一个 render-critic 细化循环，同时检查渲染结果与底层程序，将视觉缺陷追溯到具体语句并进行准确修复；系统以多代理协作方式实现模块化生成与修正。


Result: 
在大量评估中，FigTree 在图像质量和可编辑性两方面均优于现有位图方法：生成出的图既视觉高质量又便于后续精确修改；render-critic 循环显著降低了视觉缺陷并能将问题定位到程序语句以便修复。


Conclusion: 
FigTree 将科研论文的图示生成表述为递归的 SVG 程序构建问题，采用多代理系统自动将论文内容转化为结构化矢量图，并通过渲染-批评器循环在渲染层与程序层之间定位并修复视觉缺陷，从而得到高质量且可编辑的图形。


Abstract: Scientific methodology figures are essential for communicating complex methods clearly, yet creating them remains labor-intensive and typically requires multiple rounds of refinement. Recent image-generation models can synthesize visually appealing raster figures, but producing a human-satisfactory result in a single generation step remains difficult. Moreover, precise edits to raster figures are challenging for both humans and models. We formulate scientific figure generation as recursive SVG program construction and propose \textsc{FigTree}, a \textit{multi-agent} system that automatically transforms a scientific paper into a structured vector figure. \textsc{FigTree} grounds figure content in the source paper, decomposes a figure into a hierarchy of local regions, generates each region as a short SVG program, and assembles the resulting fragments. A render-critic refinement loop jointly inspects the rendered figure and its underlying program, enabling visual defects to be traced to specific statements and accurately repaired. We conduct extensive evaluations of \textsc{FigTree} on figure quality and editability, showing that \textsc{FigTree} produces high-quality figures, while also enabling more effective editing than existing raster-based methods.

</details>


### [125] [Spawn Freely, Act Sparingly: Progressive Risk Vesting for Recursive LLM-Agent Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01035)
*Molly Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为递归LLM代理引入Progressive Risk Vesting：在轨迹级别托管风险预算并按激活扣减，形式化了任意时间危害界和临界行为分析，建议在沙箱中广泛搜索、谨慎授予递归权限并收取显式风险费用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归LLM代理通过生成专业子代理来扩展搜索，但部分分支需要调用外部工具或部署代码会带来不可逆风险。需要一个原则化机制决定何时向某一分支授予不可逆行动权，既要保持搜索效率又要限制累计危害。

Method: 形式化地把风险预算托管在轨迹级别，给出对自适应生成树的任意时间（anytime）危害界证明；要求每个局部证书在条件于完整预激活历史下仍然成立（即证书条件化），并在激活门、分支计费与计算约束固定时证明延迟放权与不可逆放权在可用策略集上的等价性。构建简化的分支模型分析权威再生产数𝓡_A的临界性，使用有限类型占用模型计算风险与计算的影子价格，并通过分支计算与拆样（split-sample）合成实验展示理论现象。

Result: 证明了在适应性分支树上存在任意时间危害界，给出局部证书的条件化要求，证明延迟授予在固定激活门与预算下不损失策略空间；在简化模型中刻画了随局部风险p与权威再生产数𝓡_A变化的三相风险标度（线性、平方根、正下限）；导出了风险与计算的影子价格并在特定效用—风险结构下得到阈值授权规则；通过合成实验验证上述现象的可观测性。

Conclusion: 本文提出Progressive Risk Vesting（PRV）机制：在轨迹层面把风险预算托管并在分支获得不可逆能力时扣减，从而在自递归LLM代理的分支请求中提供可证明的任意时间危害上界，并且在激活门控、分支费用和计算约束固定的情况下，延迟授予权限能保留不可撤销收费下的可用策略。研究发现当权威再生产数𝓡_A穿越1时轨迹危害有临界行为：局部风险p→0时，次临界成比例于p，临界成比例于√p，超临界保留正下限。基于有限类型占用模型得出风险/计算影子价格，并在嵌套扇出模式下导出阈值规则。设计建议是：在沙箱内广泛搜索，谨慎授予递归权限并明确收取风险费用。

Abstract: Recursive LLM agents can broaden their search by spawning specialists. Some branches later request tools that send data or deploy code. When should a branch receive authority to act? We distinguish sandbox spawning, in which external controls prevent the specified harm, from capability activation, in which a selected branch crosses an irreversible-action boundary. Progressive Risk Vesting (PRV) holds a trajectory-level risk budget in escrow and debits it as branches are activated. We prove an anytime harm bound for adaptively generated trees. Branch outcomes may be dependent, but each local certificate needs to remain valid conditional on the full pre-activation history, including the information used to select the request. When activation gates, branch charges, and compute constraints are held fixed, delayed vesting preserves every policy available under irrevocable spawn charging. Marginal risk estimates can still fail after branch selection. In a stylized branching model, trajectory harm changes as the authority reproduction number $\mathcal{R}_A$ crosses one. As local risk $p$ approaches zero, trajectory harm is proportional to $p$ below criticality, proportional to $\sqrt{p}$ at criticality, and retains a positive floor above it. A finite-type occupancy model yields risk and compute shadow prices. For nested fanout modes with decreasing marginal value per unit risk, these prices produce a threshold rule. Branching calculations and a split-sample experiment illustrate the results. These synthetic studies do not estimate safety in deployed agents. The analysis suggests a design rule: search broadly in the sandbox and grant recursive authority sparingly, with an explicit risk charge.

</details>


### [126] [Data-Driven Persona-Conditioned Agents for A/B Test Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01038)
*Ziyad Benomar,Weronika Łajewska,Leonardo Perelli,Saab Mansour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用基于真实行为数据的画像驱动LLM代理做A/B测试仿真，系统比较多种设计要素，在40个测试上实现了0.75–0.90的方向性准确率，证明了数据驱动画像在实验预筛选中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统A/B测试耗费真实用户流量、工程资源和时间。提出一种可快速、低成本地预测实验结果的仿真方法，以便优先开展最有希望的实验。

Method: 构建以匿名行为信号（行为模式、参与度信号、推断人口统计）为条件的数据驱动画像，用这些画像初始化或约束LLM代理，把A/B测试仿真表述为结构化问答任务；系统性评估问题设计、画像数据源与领域对齐、单画像行为深度与总体多样性权衡，以及高效子采样策略。

Result: 在包含40个A/B测试且覆盖两类度量的基准上，最佳配置在不同指标上实现了0.75–0.90的方向性准确率，表明该方法在方向性预测上具有较高效用。

Conclusion: 基于数据驱动画像并由大语言模型驱动的代理能够在无需真实流量的情况下，较准确地预测A/B测试走向，为低成本实验预筛选提供可行路径。

Abstract: A/B testing is the gold standard for evaluating product changes, but each experiment requires real user traffic, engineering effort, and weeks of measurement. We propose a simulation framework that predicts A/B test outcomes using LLM-powered agents conditioned on data-driven personas grounded in real user behavioral signals. Unlike prior work that relies on synthetic or rule-based personas, our agents are constructed from anonymized behavioral data-activity patterns, engagement signals, and inferred demographics-enabling more faithful population modeling. We frame A/B test simulation as a structured question task and systematically study (i) question design formats, (ii) the impact of persona data source and domain alignment, (iii) the trade-off between per-persona behavioral depth and population diversity, and (iv) efficient population subsampling. On a benchmark of 40 A/B tests spanning two metric types, our best configuration achieves 0.75-0.90 directional accuracy depending on the test metric, demonstrating that data-driven personas are a viable path toward fast, low-cost experiment pre-screening.

</details>


### [127] [AgentFactory: Towards Automated Agentic System Design and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01045)
*Enci Zhang,Haofeng Wang,Yuesheng Zhu,Xiaole Cui,Guibo Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentFactory通过联合优化基础模型与工作流、并以LLM为优化器的三阶段管线，实现了多目标的自动化设计，显著提升agentic系统在若干基准上的性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工设计与调优agent系统成本高、可扩展性差；现有自动化方法忽略模型能力差异且通常只优化单一指标，难以满足实际部署需求。

Method: 基于先进大模型作为优化器，采用三阶段优化流水线（模型选择/微调、工作流结构搜索、联合迭代优化），在多目标（性能、成本、效率）约束下对配置空间进行自动搜索与评估。

Result: 在五个领域的八个基准上进行实验，AgentFactory相较于人工设计和已有自动方法平均提升9.1%，在医学与金融任务上分别得到19.6%和18.7%的明显增益，表明联合优化带来实质性改进。

Conclusion: 提出的AgentFactory框架能够在联合优化基础模型与工作流结构的同时兼顾性能、成本与效率，从而生成更优的agentic系统配置；在八个基准上平均提升9.1%，在领域特定任务（MedQA、FinEval）上提升显著。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities as powerful components in agentic systems, enabling sophisticated reasoning and complex task execution. However, current approaches to manually designing and optimizing agentic systems heavily rely on manual effort, limiting their adaptability and scalability. Recent work has explored the automated optimization of workflow designs. However, these approaches often overlook the crucial role of model capabilities and focus on single performance metrics, failing to address real-world deployment constraints. In this paper, we present AgentFactory, a framework that jointly optimizes both foundation models and workflow structures in agentic systems while considering multiple objectives including performance, cost, and efficiency. AgentFactory leverages advanced LLMs as optimizers to navigate the vast search space of possible configurations, employing a three-stage optimization pipeline to automatically discover effective combinations of fine-tuned models and optimized workflows. Through an iterative optimization process, our framework systematically explores and evaluates different agentic system designs, adapting to task-specific requirements while maintaining operational efficiency. We evaluate AgentFactory across eight benchmarks spanning five domains, including general reasoning, coding, mathematics, medicine, and finance. Our experiments demonstrate that AgentFactory consistently outperforms both manually designed methods and existing automated approaches, achieving an average improvement of 9.1% across all benchmarks, with particularly significant gains in domain-specific tasks (19.6% on MedQA and 18.7% on FinEval). These results establish AgentFactory as a promising approach for developing more capable and efficient agentic systems through automated optimization.

</details>


### [128] [QILP-0: Constructing Observational Declarative Twins of Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01049)
*Marina de la Cruz Echeandía,César Luis Alonso,Tony Ribeiro,Alfonso Ortega de la Puente*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This paper introduces QXymb, a general framework for constructing observational declarative twins of quantum circuits, and develops QILP-0, its first complete order-0 specialization. QILP-0 constructs a finite multi-valued propositional logic program from observed circuit behaviour within a declared observational scope.
  The pipeline traverses a declared family of quantum observables incrementally according to a reproducible structural grading and a declared observational reference horizon. Progress is quantified through reference-relative coverage against a fixed target-independent reference. Observable responses are organized through target-independent geometry, while retained latent structure is mapped deterministically back to original observable columns before symbolic processing, preserving observational semantics and provenance.
  Selected observable profiles are converted into a finite relation through admissible target-independent discretization. The target is used only afterwards to audit twin-admissibility and induce the declarative theory.
